Reporting officer bas had occasion to do considerable
flfing es a passenger in pourection with his duties. On sereral coca-

for flying daks. This phenomenon has been oberved in the vast plains
of the millanette valley and the plains of fastern dashington and Oregon,
one striking example was on the trip over the Villanette Valley on a clear
sunny day when the blue haze, alvays present, seemed somewhat thicker than
usual,

See appeas to that of at on into timed me on dald
appear, It was finally determined that what locked to be alrcraft at
various altitudes schally mers reflections of the sun from aluminn
roofs or barns and other fars buildings at great distances fron the air-
craft. This mas caused by the perspective of land converging with sky
on the horizon and the reflection of the reofs and the limited ground
visibility nade it appear as thouch these objects were actually air-
torn at verious altitudes in the haze.
